- **faithful-capture**: Capture what someone actually said without contaminating it with your own inference. Invoke when eliciting intent in conversation (design discussion, requirement interview, decision surfacing) and when turning a conversation into a durable record (ADR, decision node, memory entry, spec `## Decisions` row). Enforces quote-before-critique, keeps illustration separate from commitment, and holds interpretation subordinate to verbatim. Prevents the failure where unspecified space is filled by the recorder and then evaluated as if the speaker had specified it. ([friedbotstudio/baseline](https://github.com/friedbotstudio/baseline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/faithful-capture) · ★15)
- **faithful-doc-writing**: ユーザーの発言内容だけを忠実にドキュメント化するスキル。PRD・仕様書・企画書・ 提案書など構造化ドキュメントの作成時に使う。AIが「言ってないことを勝手に補足する」 問題を防ぐためのもの。以下のような場面で必ず発動すること: "PRDを書いて", "企画書を作って", "仕様書をまとめて", "ドキュメントにして", "草案を書いて", "叩き台を作って", "提案書を書いて", "聞いた内容をまとめて", "話した内容を文書化して", "言ったことだけ書いて", "勝手に足さないで" またユーザーの口頭説明や会話からドキュメントを起こす場面でも積極的に使うこと。 ([tktcorporation/vrchat-albums](https://github.com/tktcorporation/vrchat-albums/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/faithful-doc-writing) · ★4 · 14 copies)

- **faithful-old-photo-restoration**: 对单张老照片先看图生成专属修复提示词，再与固定通用提示词组合，执行保真人脸修复、去污去划痕、高像素导出和检查返修循环。适用于原扫描修复、已修复照片的局部改进，以及可靠参考辅助修复；不用于美颜、换脸或重新设计人物。 ([MarkCens/iterative-old-photo-restoration-skill](https://github.com/MarkCens/iterative-old-photo-restoration-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **faithful-port**: Use when porting or translating code from a cited reference into another language, framework, or engine (C++ → Rust, GLSL → WGSL, one renderer's/library's algorithm into yours) and faithfulness to the original is the goal. - **faithful-port**: Port a Java module to Rust with byte-identical output parity as the acceptance bar.
